This design system is specifically extracted and adapted for email generation based on Pomelo Fashion's Thai website design patterns:

**Key Features:**
- **Brand Colors**: Prominent red (#E31E24) as primary CTA color with full palette
- **Thai Typography**: Noto Sans Thai with proper font fallbacks for email clients
- **Button Styles**: Primary red buttons optimized for email (no JS transitions)
- **Spacing Scale**: 4px-based system works perfectly in email layouts
- **Email Safe**: Includes web-safe color variants and simplified properties

**Email Usage:**
```html
<!-- Primary Button Example -->
<a href="#" style="background: #E31E24; color: #FFFFFF; padding: 12px 24px; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; text-decoration: none; display: inline-block;">ช็อปเลย</a>
```

Ready for generating high-fidelity promotional emails matching Pomelo's brand identity!

Task 3 - Using the exact design system from task 2, create 3 different branded HTML email templates: (1) Minimalist - clean single column with lots of whitespace, (2) Bold - strong CTAs with vibrant brand colors, (3) Elegant - sophisticated premium feel with refined typography. Each template MUST include: Header with logo placeholder, Hero section with headline and CTA, Product showcase (2-3 products with image/price placeholders), Value proposition section, Footer with social links and unsubscribe. Use EXACT colors, fonts, button styles from design system. Output each template in a separate ```html code block with a comment like <!-- TEMPLATE 1: MINIMALIST --> at the start.:
```html
<!-- TEMPLATE 1: MINIMALIST -->
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="th">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Pomelo Fashion - Minimalist</title>
    <style>
        body { margin: 0; padding: 0; font-family: 'Helvetica Neue', Arial, sans-serif; background-color: #F8F9FA; }
        .email-container { max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto; background-color: #FFFFFF; }
        .header { padding: 40px 30px 20px; text-align: center; border-bottom: 1px solid #F8F9FA; }
        .logo-placeholder { width: 120px; height: 40px; background: #E31E24; margin: 0 auto 20px; display: block; }
        .hero { padding: 60px 30px; text-align: center; }
        .hero h1 { font-size: 32px; font-weight: 300; color: #2C3E50; margin: 0 0 20px; line-height: 1.3; }
        .hero p { font-size: 18px; color: #34495E; margin: 0 0 40px; }
        .cta-button { display: inline-block; padding: 16px 32px; background: #E31E24; color: #FFFFFF; text-decoration: none; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 500; border-radius: 4px; }
        .products { padding: 40px 30px; }
        .product-grid { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 20px; justify-content: center; }
        .product-card { width: 48%; max-width: 260px; text-align: center; }
        .product-image { width: 100%; height: 200px; background: #F8F9FA; margin-bottom: 15px; }
        .product-price { font-size: 20px; font-weight: 600; color: #E31E24; }
        .value-prop { padding: 60px 30px; background: #F8F9FA; text-align: center; }
        .value-prop h2 { font-size: 28px; font-weight: 300; color: #2C3E50; margin: 0 0 20px; }
        .value-prop p { font-size: 16px; color: #34495E; max-width: 500px; margin: 0 auto; line-height: 1.6; }
        .footer { padding: 40px 30px 30px; text-align: center; background: #2C3E50; color: #FFFFFF; font-size: 14px; }
        .social-links { margin: 20px 0; }
        .social-links a { color: #FFFFFF; font-size: 20px; margin: 0 10px; text-decoration: none; }
        @media (max-width: 600px) { .product-card { width: 100%; } .hero h1 { font-size: 28px; } }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="email-container">
        <div class="header">
            <div class="logo-placeholder"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="hero">
            <h1>คอลเลกชันใหม่ล่าสุด</h1>
            <p>พบกับสไตล์ที่ใช่สำหรับคุณ</p>
            <a href="#" class="cta-button">ช็อปเลย</a>

        <div class="products">
            <div class="product-grid">
                <div class="product-card">
                    <div class="product-image"></div>
                    <div class="product-price">฿1,290</div>
                </div>
                <div class="product-card">
                    <div class="product-image"></div>
                    <div class="product-price">฿2,190</div>
                </div>
                <div class="product-card">
                    <div class="product-image"></div>
                    <div class="product-price">฿890</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="value-prop">
            <h2>ทำไมต้องเลือก Pomelo</h2>
            <p>คุณภาพดี ราคาโดนใจ ส่งฟรีทั่วประเทศ รับประกันความพอใจ 100%</p>

            <p><a href="#" style="color: #FF6B6B;">ยกเลิกการรับข่าวสาร</a></p>

</body>
</html>
```

```html
<!-- TEMPLATE 2: BOLD -->
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="th">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Pomelo Fashion - Bold</title>
    <style>
        body { margin: 0; padding: 0; font-family: 'Helvetica Neue', Arial, sans-serif; background-color: #1A1A1A; }
        .email-container { max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto; background-color: #FFFFFF; }
        .header { padding: 20px 30px; text-align: center; background: linear-gradient(135deg, #E31E24, #FF6B6B); }
        .logo-placeholder { width: 140px; height: 45px; background: #FFFFFF; margin: 0 auto; display: block; border-radius: 4px; }
        .hero { padding: 50px 30px; text-align: center; background: linear-gradient(135deg, #E31E24, #FF4D4F); color: #FFFFFF; }
        .hero h1 { font-size: 36px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0 0 15px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; }
        .hero p { font-size: 20px; margin: 0 0 30px; opacity: 0.95; }
        .cta-button { display: inline-block; padding: 20px 40px; background: #F8B500; color: #2C3E50; text-decoration: none; font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; border-radius: 50px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(248,181,0,0.4); }
        .cta-button:hover { background: #FFD700; transform: translateY(-2px); }
        .products { padding: 40px 30px; background: #F8F9FA; }
        .product-grid { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 20px; }
        .product-card { width: 31%; background: #FFFFFF; border-radius: 12px; overflow: hidden; box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.1); transition: transform 0.3s; }
        .product-card:hover { transform: translateY(-5px); }
        .product-image { width: 100%; height: 180px; background: linear-gradient(45deg, #E31E24, #FF6B6B); }
        .product-info { padding: 20px; text-align: center; }
        .product-price { font-size: 24px; font-weight: 700; color: #E31E24; margin-bottom: 10px; }
        .value-prop { padding: 50px 30px; text-align: center; background: #2C3E50; color: #FFFFFF; }
        .value-prop h2 { font-size: 32px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0 0 25px; }
        .value-prop p { font-size: 18px; max-width: 500px; margin: 0 auto 30px; opacity: 0.9; }
        .footer { padding: 30px; text-align: center; background: #1A252F; color: #FFFFFF; font-size: 14px; }
        .social-links { margin: 20px 0; }
        .social-links a { display: inline-block; width: 40px; height: 40px; background: #E31E24; color: #FFFFFF; font-size: 18px; line-height: 40px; margin: 0 8px; border-radius: 50%; text-decoration: none; }
        @media (max-width: 600px) { .product-card { width: 48%; } .hero h1 { font-size: 28px; } }
        @media (max-width: 480px) { .product-card { width: 100%; } }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="email-container">
        <div class="header">
            <div class="logo-placeholder"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="hero">
            <h1>🔥 HOT DEALS!</h1>
            <p>ส่วนลดสูงสุด 70% ทุกไอเท็ม</p>
            <a href="#" class="cta-button">ช็อปด่วน!</a>
        </div>
        <div class="products">
            <div class="product-grid">
                <div class="product-card">
                    <div class="product-image"></div>
                    <div class="product-info">
                        <div class="product-price">฿1,290 <span style="font-size:14px; text-decoration:line-through; color:#999;">฿1,990</span></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="product-card">
                    <div class="product-image"></div>
                    <div class="product-info">
                        <div class="product-price">฿2,190 <span style="font-size:14px; text-decoration:line-through; color:#999;">฿3,490</span></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="product-card">
                    <div class="product-image"></div>
                    <div class="product-info">
                        <div class="product-price">฿890</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="value-prop">
            <h2>ส่งฟรี + เก็บเงินปลายทาง</h2>
            <p>สั่งวันนี้ รับภายใน 1-3 วัน ไม่ต้องรอ!</p>
            <a href="#" class="cta-button" style="background: #F8B500; color: #2C3E50;">เริ่มช็อปเลย</a>
        </div>
        <div class="footer">
            <div class="social-links">
                <a href="#">📘</a>
                <a href="#">📷</a>
                <a href="#">🐦</a>
                <a href="#">📱</a>

            <p><a href="#" style="color: #F8B500;">ยกเลิกการรับข่าวสาร</a></p>
        </div>
    </div>
</body>
</html>
```

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="th">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Pomelo Fashion - Elegant</title>
    <style>
        @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Playfair+Display:wght@400;700&family=Lora:wght@400;500&display=swap');
        body { margin: 0; padding: 0; font-family: 'Lora', serif; background-color: #F8F9FA; line-height: 1.7; }
        .email-container { max-width: 620px; margin: 0 auto; background-color: #FFFFFF; box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.1); }
        .header { padding: 50px 40px 30px; text-align: center; border-bottom: 2px solid #F8F9FA; }
        .logo-placeholder { width: 160px; height: 50px; background: linear-gradient(135deg, #E31E24, #C8101E); margin: 0 auto 20px; display: block; border-radius: 8px; }
        .hero { padding: 80px 40px; text-align: center; background: linear-gradient(135deg, #FFFFFF 0%, #F8F9FA 100%); }
        .hero h1 { font-family: 'Playfair Display', serif; font-size: 42px; font-weight: 700; color: #2C3E50; margin: 0 0 20px; line-height: 1.2; }
        .hero p { font-size: 20px; color: #34495E; margin: 0 0 50px; font-style: italic; max-width: 480px; margin-left: auto; margin-right: auto; }
        .cta-button { display: inline-block; padding: 18px 36px; background: transparent; color: #E31E24; text-decoration: none; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 500; font-family: 'Lora', serif; border: 2px solid #E31E24; border-radius: 0; letter-spacing: 1px; transition: all 0.3s; }
        .cta-button:hover { background: #E31E24; color: #FFFFFF; }
        .products { padding: 60px 40px; }
        .product-grid { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 30px; justify-content: center; }
        .product-card { width: 30%; max-width: 180px; text-align: center; }
        .product-image { width: 100%; height: 220px; background: linear-gradient(135deg, #F8F9FA, #E8E9EB); margin-bottom: 20px; border-radius: 8px; }
        .product-price { font-family: 'Playfair Display', serif; font-size: 24px; font-weight: 700; color: #2C3E50; }
        .value-prop { padding: 80px 40px; background: #1A252F; text-align: center; color: #FFFFFF; }
        .value-prop h2 { font-family: 'Playfair Display', serif; font-size: 36px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0 0 30px; }
        .value-prop p { font-size: 18px; max-width: 520px; margin: 0 auto 40px; opacity: 0.9; }
        .footer { padding: 50px 40px 40px; text-align: center; background: #FFFFFF; color: #34495E; font-size: 14px; border-top: 1px solid #F8F9FA; }
        .social-links { margin: 25px 0; }
        .social-links a { color: #E31E24; font-size: 22px; margin: 0 15px; text-decoration: none; transition: color 0.3s; }
        .social-links a:hover { color: #C8101E; }
        @media (max-width: 600px) { .product-card { width: 45%; } .hero h1 { font-size: 34px; } .products { padding: 40px 20px; } }
        @media (max-width: 480px) { .product-card { width: 100%; } }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="email-container">
        <div class="header">
            <div class="logo-placeholder"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="hero">
            <h1>คอลเลกชันเอ็กซ์คลูซีฟ</h1>
            <p>ความงามที่เรียบง่ายแต่ทรงพลัง</p>
            <a href="#" class="cta-button">ค้นพบคอลเลกชัน</a>
        </div>
        <div class="products">
            <div class="product-grid">
                <div class="product-card">
                    <div class="product-image"></div>
                    <div class="product-price">฿1,290</div>
                </div>
                <div class="product-card">
                    <div class="product-image"></div>
                    <div class="product-price">฿2,190</div>
                </div>
                <div class="product-card">
                    <div class="product-image"></div>
                    <div class="product-price">฿890</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="value-prop">
            <h2>ประสบการณ์ช็อปปิ้งที่เหนือกว่า</h2>
            <p>ทุกชิ้นงานถูกคัดสรรมาอย่างดีเยี่ยม เพื่อมอบความสมบูรณ์แบบให้กับคุณ</p>

        <div class="footer">

                <a href="#">🐦</a>

            <p><a href="#" style="color: #E31E24;">ยกเลิกการรับข่าวสาร</a></p>
```
